MacroCOSM OES

Every item of this volume as a card: its fields as the volume holds them. The app's fabula density adds facets, live examples and the local graph.

S:section/18.6.7 The moves

The dialectic's three moves are Thym's

id
S:section/18.6.7
type
section
summary
The dialectic's three moves are Thym's (Thym (https://thym.cosmoes.org/), its section 22), declared with the core's: question lands a volume's questions, from its banks and its open questions by rules and from a model where the volume is unsure, ambiguous, in conflict or silent on intent; record writes a person's answers into the questions, by code and with no model; settle folds the answered questions into the items they are about.

The taxonomy

id
S:decision/1
type
decision

MacroCOSM OES is an OES: the grammar and the core built into it.
number

provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["specs/macrocosm-oes.md#L4350-L4354"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. The taxonomy. MacroCOSM OES is an OES: the grammar and the core built into it. The core holds five built-ins, cosm/0 the product tree, canon/1 Canon, fabula/1 the fabula, doc/1 documents and thym/1 Thym's volumes, each declared by a manifest that says core: true; a built-in is never a MiniCOSM. There are two MiniCOSMs, Comprise OES and Ascribe, each with a specification of its own. What a declaration names is a format, and the grammar's nonterminal minicosm is format (sections 3, 5.1 and 18.1).

S:decision/5 The fabula's flavours and four new modules

The fabula's flavours and four new modules

id
S:decision/5
type
decision
summary
One format holds three flavours, fabulan (prose), fabulap (verse) and fabulac (code), named by the catalog's flavour:.
number
5
in
S:section/21
edition
0.6.0
dated
2026-11-06
about
S:section/18.1, S:section/18.4, S:section/18.4.6, S:section/18.4.7, S:section/18.4.8, T:term/fabula, T:term/flavour, T:term/module, T:term/format, T:term/catalog, T:term/kind, T:term/field, T:term/twin, T:term/fabulan, T:term/fabulac, T:term/cshort, T:term/catem, T:term/fabulap, T:term/item, T:term/decompile, T:term/checks
provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["specs/macrocosm-oes.md#L4368-L4378"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. The fabula's flavours and four new modules. One format holds three flavours, fabulan (prose), fabulap (verse) and fabulac (code), named by the catalog's flavour:. Four modules join L E K P T X, whose kinds, fields and closed lists are unchanged, so every older twin is a valid fabulan: I Inspiration, N Intent, C Construction and R Production. A fabulac is compiled into a cshort and told as a catem; a fabula records the model, route and provider of every run that wrote into it, a settle run included, while derived's single keys describe the run that derived it (sections 18.1 and 18.4). A fabulap gives each line, its metre and its rhymes items of their own, and lets readings of one line stand side by side (section 18.4.6). A fabulac is the specification of a program, enough to write the program again from it alone; its source folder is fingerprinted by the tree digest; a cshort is a folder of rebuilt source with its CSHORT.md and a packaging file written by rules, never by a model, checked without executing anything; and no conforming implementation runs, imports or installs a program to decompile it or a cshort to show it (section 18.4.7). The fabula's own checks are listed in section 18.4.8.

S:decision/6 The dialectic

The dialectic

id
S:decision/6
type
decision

One primitive of the core asks a person what a source means and records the answer: module D, with the kinds question and session, which every format inherits; the opening questions as banks under a manifest's dialectic: key; an entry may bound itself, only-with: <field> and at-most: <n> (section 18.6.6); and Thym's moves question, record and settle.

6

S:section/21

{"mode":"derived","sources":["specs/macrocosm-oes.md#L4379-L4387"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. The dialectic. One primitive of the core asks a person what a source means and records the answer: module D, with the kinds question and session, which every format inherits; the opening questions as banks under a manifest's dialectic: key; an entry may bound itself, only-with: <field> and at-most: <n> (section 18.6.6); and Thym's moves question, record and settle. A session lists the questions it asked, or, where a format asks from a bank without writing question items, the addresses it asked; a MiniCOSM's own text says where its volumes keep the items; a settled question whose answer removed its item is closed, not an error. An item records what an answer filled in provenance.answered, and what its subject declined in withheld. The dialectic's checks run on every volume that holds module D (sections 8.5, 10, 12.3, 12.5, 13 and 18.6).

S:decision/27 What is compiled from a twin is a SHORT, written beside the text and recorded by the twin

What is compiled from a twin is a SHORT

id
S:decision/27
type
decision
summary
One fabula, many tellings: the EMF twin, enumerated meaning fabula, is the material and a short one arrangement of it.
number
27
in
S:section/21
edition
0.0.1
dated
2026-10-23
about
T:term/twin, T:term/short, T:term/fabula, T:term/telling, T:term/atem, T:term/by-weight
provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["specs/macrocosm-oes.md#L4445-L4449"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. What is compiled from a twin is a SHORT, written beside the text and recorded by the twin. One fabula, many tellings: the EMF twin, enumerated meaning fabula, is the material and a short one arrangement of it. The author's text is the first arrangement and is never called a short; the atem stays outside the pair. Every compile is a new file in one of two orders, the author's or by weight, and the twin lists its shorts so the material knows its tellings.

S:decision/28 A document is enumerated in its own grammar

A document is enumerated in its own grammar

id
S:decision/28
type
decision
summary
DocOES (doc/1) takes a markdown document apart into its sections, terms, examples and decisions and joins them to the reference generated from the manifests; compiled as told, the volume gives the markdown back byte for byte.
number
28
in
S:section/21
edition
0.0.1
dated
2026-10-23
about
T:term/docoes, T:term/reference, T:term/manifest, T:term/volume, T:term/item, T:term/atem
provenance
{"mode":"derived","sources":["specs/macrocosm-oes.md#L4450-L4454"],"confidence":1,"state":"known"}
  1. A document is enumerated in its own grammar. DocOES (doc/1) takes a markdown document apart into its sections, terms, examples and decisions and joins them to the reference generated from the manifests; compiled as told, the volume gives the markdown back byte for byte. The markdown stays the authoring surface, the volume is built at runtime and never committed, every item carries a one-line atem made by rule, and this specification's own tables are the hand-written overlay on what is generated, so nobody edits a generated item.

{"about":"what the question is about: an item id; an item id, # and a field path (section 11.2), N:audience/owners#who, which may point at a field that is absent or pending; a span of the source ({lines}, {start, end}, or {file, lines} in code); or, for a bank question asked of the volume with no item to attach to, the module (I) or the kind (N:audience) its bank entry names","asks":"the question, in plain words and in no one's voice, {name} already filled","why":"why it is asked, from a closed list: missing (nobody has said) · unsure (an item below confidence 0.5, or pending) · ambiguous (two readings stand) · intent (was it meant) · conflict (two answers or two items disagree) · counter (a check refused an answer; asked again differently)","kind":"the answer's shape, from a closed list: text · choice · yes-no · number · date · ref · span","options":"for choice, the choices (required); for ref, the id prefixes the answer may name (optional)","options-open":"true when a choice also takes a word outside options","many":"true when the answer is a list","range":"[low, high], for number","hint":"a line shown under the question","to":"who can answer: author · expert · maker · holder (Ascribe's subject) · owner (Comprise's item owner) · a role's id · anyone","raised":"{by, from, at, run}: by is bank, move, model or person; from the bank entry (fabulap/speaker), the move or the stage that raised it; at a timestamp; run the run's trace id","state":"open · answered · unknown · declined · stale · withdrawn (section 18.6.3)","answers":"the answers, appended and never rewritten: each {at, by, says, value, confidence, session, declined}. by is a word of to or a role's id; says the person's words, verbatim; value what is written (null for \"I don't know\" and for a refusal); confidence how sure the person is, 0 to 1, and 1 for a value given with none (absent on \"I don't know\" or a refusal unless the person gave one); session the session's id; declined: true on a refusal","settled":"{at, run, wrote}, written by settle: when, in which run, and the addresses it wrote ([N:audience/owners#who]). Absent until then","ended":"{at, by, because}, for stale and withdrawn"}
declared

R:check/question-about question-about

id
R:check/question-about
type
check
summary
The question-about check, a check of the core's dialectic: an about that names nothing in the volume: an item that does not exist, a field path that does not parse, a span of the wrong shape (in a fabulac, one whose file or lines do not exist), a module or kind the format does not declare. A question that has settled, or whose state is stale or withdrawn, is skipped, since its answer may have removed what it was about, or it has ended because what it was about is gone.

error
